  68. Kelsky, K. (2015). The Professor Is In: The Guide to Turning your PhD into a Job (A Winning Grant Template, Part VIII).
  69. Zuckerman, E. W. (n.d.). On Genre: A Few More Tips to Article-Writers.
  70. Belcher, W. L. (n.d.). Writing Your Journal Article in 12 Weeks: A Guide to Academic Success (Advancing your argument). Thousand Oaks: Sage, 67-98.
